  <script>
    // Lazy loading do rybena.js - carrega apenas ao interagir com botões de acessibilidade
    (function () {
      let rybenaScriptLoaded = false;
      let rybenaScriptLoading = false;
      let pendingClick = null;

      function loadRybenaScript(clickEvent) {
        if (rybenaScriptLoaded) {
          // Script já carregado, permite a interação natural
          return true;
        }

        if (!rybenaScriptLoading) {
          rybenaScriptLoading = true;

          const script = document.createElement('script');
          script.src = './assets/js/rybena.js';

          script.onload = function () {
            rybenaScriptLoaded = true;
            rybenaScriptLoading = false;

            // Força inicialização do RybenaDOM
            if (typeof RybenaDOM !== 'undefined' && RybenaDOM.getInstance) {
              try {
                RybenaDOM.getInstance();
              } catch (e) {
                console.log('RybenaDOM já inicializado ou erro:', e);
              }
            }

            // Aguarda um pouco para garantir que tudo foi inicializado
            setTimeout(function () {
              // Após carregar, simula o clique novamente se houver um pendente
              if (pendingClick) {
                const element = pendingClick.target;
                const newEvent = new MouseEvent('click', {
                  bubbles: true,
                  cancelable: true,
                  view: window
                });
                element.dispatchEvent(newEvent);
                pendingClick = null;
              }
            }, 200);
          };

          script.onerror = function () {
            console.error('Erro ao carregar rybena.js');
            rybenaScriptLoading = false;
          };

          document.head.appendChild(script);
        }

        // Armazena o evento para reprocessar após o carregamento
        if (clickEvent && !rybenaScriptLoaded) {
          pendingClick = clickEvent;
          return false;
        }

        return false;
      }

      // Intercepta cliques nos elementos de acessibilidade
      document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        const target = event.target;

        // Verifica se o clique foi em algum elemento relacionado ao Rybena
        if (target.closest('.btn-rybena-sign-language') ||
          target.closest('.btn-rybena-voice') ||
          target.closest('.btn-rybena-vision') ||
          target.closest('.custom-rybena__box')) {

          if (!rybenaScriptLoaded) {
            // Previne o comportamento padrão até o script carregar
            event.stopPropagation();
            event.preventDefault();
            loadRybenaScript(event);
            return false;
          }
        }
      }, true);
    })();
  </script>
  <div class="custom-rybena" id="custom-rybena-div" style="">
    <ul class="custom-rybena__box">
      <li class="custom-rybena__box__bt btn-rybena-sign-language">
        <div>
          <span> Libras </span>

    <script>
      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
        let cmsLoaded = false;

        function showRybenaBar() {
          const rybenaDiv = document.getElementById("custom-rybena-div");
          if (rybenaDiv) {
            rybenaDiv.style.display = "";
          }
        }

        window.addEventListener("cmsContentLoaded", function () {
          cmsLoaded = true;
          showRybenaBar();
        });

        setTimeout(function () {
          if (!cmsLoaded) {
            showRybenaBar();
          }
        }, 5000);

        const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
          const rybena = document.querySelector(".custom-rybena");
          if (!rybena) {
            return;
          }
          if (document.body.classList.contains("popup-close")) {
            rybena.classList.add("menu-hidden");
          } else {
            rybena.classList.remove("menu-hidden");
          }
        });

      observer.observe(document.body, {
        attributes: true,
        attributeFilter: ["class"],
      });

        const rybenaToggleBtn = document.querySelector(".custom-rybena__btn");
        if (rybenaToggleBtn) {
          rybenaToggleBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
            const rybena = document.querySelector(".custom-rybena");
            if (rybena) {
              rybena.classList.toggle("menu-hidden");
            }
          });
        }
      });

    let rybenaIframes = [];
    function addRybenaEvents(elem) {
      let doc = elem.contentDocument;
      if (doc === null || hasRybena(doc) || rybenaIframes.includes(doc))
        return;

      RybenaLog.getInstance().debug("Identified new iframe");
      rybenaIframes.push(doc);

      doc.addEventListener("mouseup", (event) => {
        mouseUpEvent(event, doc);
      });

      doc.addEventListener("message", (event) => {
        messageEvent(event, doc);
      });

      doc.addEventListener("mousemove", (event) => {
        mouseMove(event);
      });

      getIframes(doc).map((d) => {
        addRybenaEvents(d);
      });
    }

    function getIframes(doc) {
      return Array.from(doc.getElementsByTagName("iframe"));
    }

    function handleRybenaEvent() {
      getIframes(document).map((d) => {
        addRybenaEvents(d);
      });
    }

    function hasRybena(doc) {
      return Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll("script")).some((script) =>
        script.src.match(/rybena\.js/gm),
      );
    }

    function mouseUpEvent(event, doc) {
      let text = "";
      event &&
        "string" == typeof event.target.alt &&
        (text += event.target.alt),
        doc.getSelection
          ? (text += "" + doc.getSelection())
          : doc.selection && (text += "" + doc.selection.createRange().text),
        null != text &&
        text.trim().length > 0 &&
        ((text = text.trim() || ""),
          parent.postMessage("##iframe##" + text, "*"));
    }

    function mouseMove(e) {
      parent.postMessage(
        {
          name: "MOUSE_MOVEMENT",
          x: e.movementX,
          y: e.movementY,
        },
        "*",
      );
    }

    function messageEvent(event) {
      if (
        event.data &&
        "string" == typeof event.data &&
        event.data.includes("##iframe##")
      )
        try {
          parent.postMessage(event.data, "*");
        } catch (e) {
          console.error("ERROR ON PROPAGATE EVENT TO PARENT");
        }
    }

      handleRybenaEvent();
    </script>

    <script>
      (function() {
        let dayaneLoaded = false;

        function limparScriptsDayane() {
          document
            .querySelectorAll('script[src*="dayane-new.js"]')
            .forEach(function (script) {
              script.remove();
            });
        }

        function resetarEstadoSdkDayane() {
          if (typeof window.DayaneChatbotReset === 'function') {
            window.DayaneChatbotReset();
          }
          window.__DayaneChatbotInitialized__ = false;
          window.dayaneCarregando = false;
          limparScriptsDayane();
        }

        function loadDayane() {
          var widgetExistente = document.getElementsByClassName("rcb-chat-tooltip-tail")[0];
          if (dayaneLoaded) {
            if (widgetExistente instanceof HTMLElement) {
              widgetExistente.click();
              return;
            }
            dayaneLoaded = false;
          }

          resetarEstadoSdkDayane();

          // Em /atendimento o SDK espera pedidoDados e não monta o chat sem bypass
          var path = (window.location.pathname || '').toLowerCase();
          if (path.indexOf('/atendimento') !== -1) {
            window.DayanePedidoDados = {
              ehPaginaAtendimento: function () { return false; },
              montar: function (opts) { if (opts && opts.aoAbrir) opts.aoAbrir(); },
              abrirJanelaEscondendoIcone: function () {}
            };
          }

          // Configuração da Dayane
          window.DayaneNewConfig = {
            guidCanal: "1D28A9CE-A6F8-4258-81E6-C917A562B231",
            baseUrl: "https://www.daycoval.com.br//dayane",
            metadados: [ ],
            parametersSdk: {
              header: "Portal Daycoval"
            }
          };

          // Carrega o script da Dayane dinamicamente
          const script = document.createElement('script');
          script.src = "https://www.daycoval.com.br/sdkdayane/scripts/dayane-new.js";
          script.async = true;

          script.onload = function() {

            // Aguarda o elemento da Dayane aparecer no DOM
            const checkDayaneLoaded = setInterval(function() {
              const element = document.getElementsByClassName("rcb-chat-tooltip-tail")[0];

              if (element instanceof HTMLElement) {
                clearInterval(checkDayaneLoaded);

                // Clica no elemento para abrir o chat
                element.click();

                // Remove o botão após abrir
                const btn = document.getElementById('dayane-trigger-btn');
                if (btn) {
                  btn.style.display = 'none';
                }
              }
            }, 100); // Verifica a cada 100ms

            // Timeout de segurança: para de verificar após 10 segundos
            setTimeout(function() {
              clearInterval(checkDayaneLoaded);
              if (!document.getElementsByClassName("rcb-chat-tooltip-tail")[0]) {
                dayaneLoaded = false;
                resetarEstadoSdkDayane();
              }
            }, 10000);
          };

          document.body.appendChild(script);
          dayaneLoaded = true;

        }

        window.__resetDayaneTrigger = function() {
          dayaneLoaded = false;
          resetarEstadoSdkDayane();
        };

        // Adiciona evento de clique ao botão
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
          const btn = document.getElementById('dayane-trigger-btn');
          if (btn) {
            btn.addEventListener('click', loadDayane);
          }
        });
      })();
    </script>

        var ie = ua.search(/(MSIE|Trident)/);
        if (ie > -1 === true) {

        }
      }
      detectIE();
    });
